The Web build plays on phones and tablets in landscape (mobile Chrome / Safari, the itch.io embed); desktop keyboard / mouse / controller behaviour is unchanged; native Android / iOS apps stay deferred. **Detection** (`TouchSupport`, data in `touch_data.gd`): a device whose primary pointer is a finger (`pointer: coarse`, or touch points with no fine pointer, which catches iPadOS's desktop user agent) is a phone / tablet; never under the autotest or headless. **Input device:** `InputHints` gains a third device, TOUCH (no chips at all; a finger is decisive, Godot's mouse events emulated from a finger are not, a key / pad press switches back); a phone / tablet starts on TOUCH, any other device switches on its first real touch. **Touch controls** (`TouchControls`, added by `Game` outside the autotest, shown only on TOUCH while the run is PLAYING; every size in CSS px, converted by `TouchSupport.layout_per_css()`): a floating joystick on the left half (radius 52 CSS px, dead zone 14%, the stick drifts after the thumb past 1.35× the radius) that presses the existing `move_*` actions with a strength lifted past their own 0.2 dead zone, so `Necromancer` keeps its one `Input.get_vector()` path; on the right Grave Step (68 CSS px face, 46 px hit radius; recharge sweep and charge pips), Dread Rally (soul-meter fill, green border when ready, violet during the rally), the Shadow Bolt aim toggle (only with the bolt) and a four-way stance rose (Escort up, Onslaught right, Spearhead down, Bone Ring left: the controller's right-stick flick directions; a finger anywhere in a quarter of the 60 px rose picks it, sliding re-picks after the lockout); pause in the top-right corner (the HUD's top-right block moves 62 CSS px left, `Hud.set_top_right_inset()`). Every button injects its existing action as an `InputEventAction` (press on finger down, release on finger up), so `Game._unhandled_input()`, `ArmyStance` and `PauseMenu` keep every rule; fingers are tracked by index (multi-touch); everything is released when the controls hide. Octagonal faces (cut corners) in the kit's violet on two layers, idle at 42% and held at 80% opacity. **Menus by tapping:** Godot's touch-to-mouse emulation covers buttons, cards, tabs and sliders (checked); the Codex lists scroll by dragging from any tile (`TouchSupport.pass_drags()`: their buttons pass mouse events to the ScrollContainer, which cancels the press past its dead zone; inert without a touchscreen); hover-only details became tap details: the Ossuary buys on a **second** tap of the same tile on TOUCH (the first shows its details). **Mobile UI scale:** a phone in landscape is 360-430 CSS px tall, so the 1280×720 layout showed body text at about 9.5 CSS px and 42 px buttons at about 21 CSS px. On a window at most 540 CSS px tall `Window.content_scale_factor` grows up to ×1.2 while at least 1280×600 layout px stay visible (Pixel 7 landscape, 863×360 CSS px: ×1.2, body text about 11.4 CSS px); tablets keep the desktop layout (iPad 1080×810: ×1, text already at 0.8×). Screens were laid out for 720 px, so `TouchSupport.fit_to_screen()` shrinks a screen's centred content (or the title column, from its top) just enough when it would run off the visible area (measured: a tall level-up card and the Codex panel); it never triggers at the desktop's 720 px layout. **Portrait:** `RotateOverlay` ("Turn your device", two phone outlines, layer 128, eats taps) covers a phone / tablet held upright and Main pauses a running game; the page asks `screen.orientation.lock('landscape')` when it enters fullscreen (the only time a browser allows it; iOS has neither). **Web page** (`web/mobile_shell.html`, `html/custom_html_shell`): the stock Godot shell plus no pinch / double-tap zoom, fixed body without scroll bounce or pull-to-refresh, no callout or tap highlight, `touch-action: none` on the canvas, every AudioContext resumed on the first touchend / pointerup / click / key (iOS counts only those as gestures), and the landscape lock; no `viewport-fit=cover`, so a notch letterboxes rather than covering the HUD. **Effects setting** (Settings → Video, `EffectsData`, `[video] effects`): Full / Reduced, Reduced by default on a phone / tablet (Full elsewhere and always in the autotest); Reduced spawns 40% of the cosmetic particles (FxLayer, its own RNG), draws only the big damage numbers (40+ or boss hits), stops drawing cosmetic Effects past 70 live (still pooled, stepped and counted; telegraphs always draw) and halves the screen shake: purely visual (checked: an army autotest with Reduced forced on gives the identical summary). The Video page grew to six rows (306 px). **Measured** in headless Chromium with SwiftShader through Playwright's device emulation (Pixel 7 landscape 863×360 at DPR 2.625, iPad landscape 1080×810 at DPR 2, desktop 1280×720), driven by real taps and synthetic multi-touch: title → stage select → pre-run → run by tapping, joystick move with a simultaneous dash, a level-up card, pause, a settings slider and the Effects row, the stance rose and aim button, the Codex drag-scroll, the Ossuary two-tap, the portrait overlay with the auto-pause, the audio resuming on the first tap; no console errors; the desktop viewport shows no touch controls and its key chips. Not measured on real phones (iOS Safari audio unlock and performance first). itch.io: tick "Mobile friendly" with orientation "Landscape" in the embed options. |
| — | Empowered grave and crypt art | Owner playtest (Oct 2026): "our empowered graves still are using placeholder assets". The StageDirector shrines and the consecration grave were the last code-drawn props in a run. Five pixel sprites (`structures/`), each named by its stage data's new `sprite` key so every stage keeps its own look: `empowered_grave` (Graveyard, 40 px), `battlefield_dead` (Crimson March, 36 px), `sanctum_crypt` (Sanctum, 36 px), `crypt_door` (`GraveyardStage.CRYPT_DEFENDER`, 56 px) and `consecration_grave` (`GraveyardStage.CONSECRATION`, 30 px). `StageDirector` draws the art through `SpriteBank.draw()` (shrines fade through the tint's alpha in their last seconds) and keeps its code glyphs as the fallback; the claim ring, the necrotic glow, the reward icon (now placed above the sprite) and the consecration's gold ring and channel arc stay drawn in code. Visual only: gameplay and autotest summaries unchanged. ~$0.13 of image generation. |
| — | Placeholder art batch | Owner request (Oct 2026): "finish the batch" of code-drawn placeholders after the empowered graves. Every remaining code-drawn marker, overlay and spell prop a player sees in a run, and four icons the code asked for without a PNG, got pixel art the same way: each site names its key in `MarkerArtData` (`scripts/data/marker_art_data.gd`, with the placement numbers), draws it through `SpriteBank` and keeps its code drawing as the fallback; rings, telegraphs, zones, glows and progress arcs stay drawn in code. Keys: `markers/rally_pennant` (boss Call to Arms), `overlays/frozen_shell` (Stasis / Deep Freeze; a whole-number scale from the body radius: ×1 on soldiers, ×2 on the bosses, so bosses get a shell their size with even pixels), `markers/buff_chevron`, `markers/sun_ward`, `markers/mage_mark`, `markers/death_brand` (+ `_doom`), `markers/stun_stars` (a still: overlays redraw only when a unit's look changes), `markers/sanctuary_cross` (Seraphine's nova safe spot), `decals/corpse`, `_2`, `_3` (Corpse Explosion; variant = the corpse's pool slot, no RNG), `structures/bone_wall_segment` / `_bastion` (they grow out of the ground as they rise and sink as they crumble through the new `SpriteBank.draw_rising()`; odd segments mirrored), `projectiles/corpse_boulder` / `_burning` (the catapult's skull, upright, larger high in its arc), `projectiles/oracle_shard`, `projectiles/eclipse_orb`, `summons/cathedral_horror_stone` (the stone form: the Cathedral Horror's own still turned to grey stone, replacing the code stone shell; the Crypt Gargoyle has no stone form), `icons/heal`, `icons/grave_fortitude`, `icons/call_of_the_grave` (the fallback level-up cards), `icons/tree_oath` (a copy of the Profane Oath badge, the Oath tree's only upgrade) and `ui/rotate_phone` (the portrait overlay's phone). `SpriteBank.draw_flat()` gained an optional scale. Visual only: gameplay, RNG and autotest summaries unchanged. 10 images, ~$0.20 of image generation (markers batched several to a sheet). |
| — | Stall recall for wedged summons | Found by the pre-content audit (Oct 2026): a Warrior returning to the player was pinned for the rest of the run between a pine and a Crimson March watchtower (Crimson March, seed 6, summon bot, Abomination + Warrior, synergies off). The arena's push-out slid it around the pine into the tower, and a standing structure takes no share of the body-block push (`Enemy.resolve_blocking()`), so the two corrections cancelled every tick: no slide rule frees such a pocket. Fix: `SummonedUnit._check_stall()` in `think()`: a unit that is RETURNING, or FOLLOWING more than `UnitData.STALL_MIN_GAP` (48 px) from its post, and moves less than `STALL_DISTANCE` (12 px) in `STALL_TIME` (3 s) gets the existing hard recall's teleport to its post (`_safe_recall()`); counted in `HordeDirector.stall_recalls` (also a hard recall) and the autotest summons line, with a `stall recall:` log line. Deterministic (positions and game time). It fires only on wedged units: the default, `--mortal`, army and Crimson March runs are unchanged; the Sanctum army run caught one Warrior pinned on a pillar 454 px out, which changes that run from there on. |
| — | Pre-content balance check (Oct 2026) | After the figure redesign, the newer-class synergies, the Sister of the Scourge, the Web / mobile builds and the art batches, a full 16-seed check (`--mortal`, fresh save, seeds 1-16, army and naive bots on all three stages, 96 runs) against the previous full batch (after the 0.5 card weights): **no regression**, 0 errors and 0 stuck units in all 96. Army bot: Graveyard identical (1 late death at 17:23, Alaric 15/16, kill 1:24, min HP 103); Crimson March improved (0 deaths, Vael 15/16 from 11/16, 1:28, min HP 93); Sanctum 1 death (from 2), Seraphine 13/16 (unchanged, 1:08, min HP 77). The army bot's drop from 15/16 to 13/16 Seraphine kills dates from the 0.5 card weights (it never drafts the newer items, so their cards are dead draws for it); the owner chose then to strengthen the newer summons rather than lower the weights. Naive bot deaths stay above the 20-40% target (Graveyard 9/16, March 11/16, Sanctum 15/16), accepted until the final balance pass, which keeps these numbers as its baseline. |
| — | Web audio buffer patch | Playtest bug (Oct 2026): on phones the browser game "eventually resets", as if it hit a cache limit. Measured: the native build holds steady (113 → ~130 MB over a run, 123 MB at every one of a dozen restarts, the arena cell table bounded, no orphan nodes), and so does the browser's WASM heap (138 MB). The leak is in Godot 4.3's Web sample playback: `GodotAudio.Sample.getAudioBuffer()` returns `_duplicateAudioBuffer()`, so every sound played (and every loop restart) copies the sample's whole stereo float buffer twice, a Float32Array per channel and then a new AudioBuffer. In headless Chromium at real speed that was 183 MB of AudioBuffers in the first 4 minutes (1,855 plays, about 0.8 MB/s, more later in a run). Desktop browsers collect it; a phone tab (iOS Safari first) is killed for memory and reloads. Fix: `tools/patch_web_audio.py` rewrites the accessor in the exported `index.js` to return the sample's one buffer (Web Audio lets one AudioBuffer feed any number of source nodes, and Godot never writes to it after registering it). CI runs it after the Web export and fails if the engine script no longer matches; local Web exports must run it too (README, CLAUDE.md). Same run after the patch: 6 MB of AudioBuffers in total (the registered samples, once each), the same 1,855 plays, no page errors. Engine and game code are unchanged; desktop builds are untouched. Recheck (or drop) the patch when the engine is upgraded. |
| — | iPhone sound and fullscreen | Playtest bug (Oct 2026, owner on iPhone Safari): sound has never worked on the phone, and the Fullscreen toggle does nothing. **Fullscreen:** iPhone Safari has no page Fullscreen API (`document.fullscreenEnabled` is false; only videos go fullscreen; iPad has it), so the toggle cannot work there. `Settings.fullscreen_mode()` asks the page once (`TouchData.FULLSCREEN_QUERY`, `STANDALONE_QUERY`, `EMBEDDED_QUERY`): with the API the toggle stays (desktop, Android, iPad); without it the Video tab shows a dim BODY line instead, "Fullscreen: Share, Add to Home Screen" when the game is the page itself (the shell's `apple-mobile-web-app-capable` makes a home-screen launch fullscreen, with its own saves), "Fullscreen: not in this browser" when embedded in another page (the itch.io page on an iPhone), and nothing in a home-screen launch (already fullscreen). **Sound** (in `web/mobile_shell.html`; the game and Godot are unchanged): (1) Safari plays Web Audio as "ambient" sound, which the ring / silent switch mutes, so the page sets `navigator.audioSession.type = "playback"` (Safari 16.4+): the game is heard in silent mode and, like a video, pauses other apps' music; older iOS starts a looping silent `<audio>` element in the first gesture for the same effect. (2) Every gesture resumes any context that is not running (iOS also uses "interrupted", after a call, Siri, the lock screen or another app, which Godot's own resume ignores) and starts one silent frame in it (older iOS unlocks only on a sound started in the gesture). (3) On Apple touch devices Godot's AudioContext is created without its 44.1 kHz `sampleRate` option, at the device's own rate (48 kHz); Godot reads the real rate back and passes playbackRate 1 for its samples, so pitch is unchanged. Checked in Chromium with iPhone emulation (the note row, the unlock and the silent-loop path, no page errors) and desktop (toggle and sound unchanged: the bus gains carry signal during a run); the real fix can only be confirmed on an iPhone. |
| — | Save backup codes and the GitHub Pages build | Playtest bug (Oct 2026, owner on iPhone Safari): "my progress keeps getting reset". Cause: on itch.io the game runs in a frame from another site (html-classic.itch.zone inside itch.io), and Safari gives such a frame partitioned storage that lasts only until Safari closes; IndexedDB (Godot's `user://`) goes with it, along with the whole profile. No in-page setting fixes that (the Storage Access API covers cookies only), so the owner chose (1) **a page of its own**: the optional CI job `publish-pages-repo` force-pushes each `main` / `v*` Web build as one commit to a separate **public** repository that holds only the build (variable `PAGES_REPO`, secret `PAGES_TOKEN`: a fine-grained token with Contents write on that repository only), served by GitHub Pages from its `main` root, since Pages on the private source repository needs a paid plan (the existing `publish-pages` job stays for that case). As a top-level site its saves persist in Safari, and **Add to Home Screen** opens it fullscreen (the Settings line from "iPhone sound and fullscreen" says so there). The shell also asks for persistent storage (`navigator.storage.persist()`). And (2) **save codes**: Settings gets a fourth tab, **Save** (Backup / Restore, `SaveBackup` in `scripts/core/save_backup.gd`, words in `save_backup_data.gd`). A code is `NECRO1:` + base64 of the gzipped `user://save.cfg` (about 1,000 characters: Soul Ash, upgrades, unlocks, records, recipes, Codex; settings and key bindings stay per device). Backup writes Meta first, then tries the browser clipboard and falls back to `window.prompt()` showing the code (a prompt needs no user gesture, so it works from Godot's main loop and inside the itch.io frame); on desktop it uses the system clipboard. Restore reads a code from a prompt (desktop: the clipboard), checks it (prefix, base64 alphabet, gzip, a ConfigFile with `[meta] save_version`; at most 1 MB unpacked), asks first (ConfirmDialog, Cancel focused), writes it like `Meta.save()` (temp file + rename) and reloads it through `Meta.load_save()`, the one load path (older codes migrate like older saves). It is refused during a run (the menu says to restore from the main menu) and under the autotest (`Meta.can_write()`). Checked: a headless round trip, and in Chromium the Save tab, Backup's prompt fallback, a crafted 777 Soul Ash code restored to the title screen, and an invalid code refused; the default, `--mortal` and army autotests are unchanged. |
| — | Bullet heaven pass | Owner request (Oct 2026): "a bit more bullet heaven vibe", with summons firing flashier attacks; built to try out, kept or dropped after the owner plays it. Proposal doc answers: volleys switch on at **Lv 3**; **denser fodder yes**; the **Rally Barrage on top of Rally's bonuses (no trade)**; **no Crescendo**; **all 12 spells**; **before Act II**. One switch for the whole pass: **Settings → Video → Volleys** (`Settings.volleys`, default on, saved under `[feel]`), read once per run as `Game.volleys_on`; autotest `--volleys=on|off` (default on). With it off nothing of the pass runs (`--volleys=off` reproduced the same-seed summaries of the game before the pass exactly; since "Bone Ring is the only formation" it is the ring game without volleys). `VolleyDirector` (`scripts/horde/volley_director.gd`, numbers in `volley_data.gd`) owns every shot as plain data in packed arrays (no node per shot; one `_draw()`, additive glow, drawn under the Covenant's projectiles and telegraphs, in the necromancer's violets / greens / bone / frost, never crimson or gold), moved without physics and collided through the enemy spatial hash; every hit goes through `CombatResolver.damage_enemy()` with the firing unit's own `dmg_source` (or the spell id), so synergies, crits, armour and the per-class statistics count it as that class's. **Summon volleys:** each class's signature volley from class Lv 3, a second count at Lv 5, and each summon evolution's showpiece (Death Knight sword wave, Gravebow arrow rain, Lich spiral, Profane Bishop ring, Bone Colossus double ring, Shadow Reaper dagger storm, Cathedral Horror shard ring, Winter Revenant blizzard swirl, Hellhound burning wolves, Keening Lady rings, Ossuary Engine burning fragments). Attack-triggered classes fire every Nth attack release (`SummonedUnit._step_cast()` → `VolleyDirector.on_attack()`); the specials fire from their own moment (Abomination stomp, Rogue dash end, Gargoyle dive, Banshee Dirge wave, catapult boulder landing, mage orb burst). A shot carries a share of the triggering hit (the volley splits damage); one volley lands at most 2 hits on bosses and minibosses. **Spell echoes:** every owned spell from Lv 3 releases a themed echo volley on its own cooldown (a uniform layer rather than a rewrite of each spell; counts for Lv 3-4 / Lv 5 / evolved). **Rally Barrage:** Dread Rally fires every summon's volley at once (Lv 1-2 classes at the Lv 3 count) and attack volleys come twice as often for the 8 s buff. **Denser fodder:** spawn rate and caps ×1.25, every non-boss enemy's HP ×0.8 (`DifficultyModifiers.enemy_spawn_mult()` / `enemy_hp_mult()`); kills keep their full XP (owner, Oct 2026: "I don't want the xp limited. The build being finished early is fine"; an ×0.8 XP cut was tried and reverted, and the check below was measured with it). Shot cap 320 on every platform (identical play everywhere); Effects = Reduced draws at most 160. First look (screenshots at 10:00 and 16:40): shots died before reaching the screen edge, so every life ×1.6, drawn ×2.3, and the frequent classes fire more often with smaller shares. Check (Graveyard, `--mortal`, fresh save, 8 seeds, on vs off): army bot 8/8 victories both, min HP 93 vs 117, Alaric 8/8 both (median kill 88 s vs 86 s), build complete 17:05 vs 16:30, Death Knight 8:00-14:07 vs 9:37-14:13, kills +21%, level at 10:00 34.2 vs 35.0, last-minute step time 2.90 vs 2.62 ms; naive bot 4/8 vs 5/8 victories, Alaric 3/8 vs 1/8. 0 errors, 0 stuck units. Not yet measured on a real phone. |
| — | Bone Ring is the only formation | Owner decision (Oct 2026): "formations was cool … but it's not really hitting like I want. Can we make bone ring the way they're supposed to be? That way it might be easier to implement some of the bullet heaven stuff"; answer to the follow-up question: **the ring is the only formation**. Supersedes "Army stances". The army always stands in the **Bone Ring** around the necromancer and its volleys fly outward in every direction. Posts (`StanceFormations`, every formation tick): blockers and flankers ("front" and "flank" roles) interleaved proportionally with the ranged units on one closed circle, evenly spaced from the top with a small jitter and a ±6 px depth wobble; the support units (priests, Corpse Catapults) in a band inside it (at least 24 px from the necromancer and 30 px inside the ring). Ring radius = posts × 24 px ÷ 2π, at least 60 px plus 3 px per inner unit, at most **150 px**; an army too big for 150 px stands **two deep** (every second post 22 px inward). Chase leash **210 px** (times Bound Souls), soft recall 320 px, formation pull ×1, hard recall unchanged (650 px). The ring's −10% army damage taken stays **off** (`BONUSES_ENABLED`). Removed: the stance keys 1–4 and their input actions (saved bindings for them are ignored), right-stick flicks, the 1.5 s lockout, the HUD stance bar, the banner and command horn, the ground outline, the pause stance tiles, the touch stance rose, the stance icons, `--stance=` and the autotest stance bot (every policy uses the ring). **Spearhead Doctrine** left the Ossuary: a save that owns it gets its **200 Soul Ash** back once on load (`MetaData.RETIRED_REFUNDS`, `MetaProgress.retired_refund`); everything now costs 9,635 (10,010 with the Oath). The `stance_command` sound stays in the synth list (removing it would shift the seeds of the sounds after it). The autotest's "army formation" line reports the ring's time, kills, army damage taken, hard recalls and spread. Check (Graveyard, `--mortal`, fresh save, volleys on, full XP, 8 seeds; against the bullet heaven check with Escort and the XP cut): army bot 8/8 victories, min HP 114 (was 93), Alaric 8/8 (median kill 61 s, was 88 s), build complete 15:36 (was 17:05), evolutions median 13:45 (was 16:19), kills 5,519 (was 5,480); naive bot 7/8 victories (was 4/8), Alaric 6/8 (was 3/8), min HP 22. The naive bot now sits below its 20–40% death target; accepted for now (the ring was asked for to make the bullet heaven play better, and the final balance pass owns the numbers). 0 errors, 0 stuck units, 0 hard recalls. |
| — | Volley art | Owner request (Oct 2026): "do an asset run as well … so we can see it in a more final state. I'd like the effects to be somewhat flashy and eye catching." Seven shot masks (`assets/sprites/volleys/`: shard, orb, dagger, spark, note, feather, wave) and two 6-frame strips (`effects/volley_hit`, an impact starburst on every volley hit, and `effects/volley_flash`, a rune-ring launch flash on every volley fired, at the landing point for a rain). The art is **grey tint masks**, not palette art: one image per shape serves every class, evolution and spell echo, multiplied by the shot's colour (lightened 30% toward white) and drawn additively with linear filtering, like the other smooth effects. Shots keep a small glow halo (at most 12 px) under the mask; impacts draw at 90% opacity, flashes at 45% (at full opacity a 45-unit ring firing at once washed out to white). Bursts live in a 96-slot ring buffer inside `VolleyDirector`, timed on game time; Effects = Reduced draws at most 24. Gargoyle and Cathedral Horror volleys now use the **feather** look (stone feather darts; the look only). Drawing only: nothing is loaded or recorded headless, and no gameplay number or RNG is touched (same-seed autotest summaries unchanged). Generated with `tools/gen_image.py`, converted with the new `tools/volley_art.py`; 9 images, about $0.29 (ART_BIBLE "Volley shots and bursts"). |
| — | Act II: the Ancient Eye (owner decisions) | Owner answers in the proposal doc "Necropathy: The Drowned Choir (three new stages)" (Oct 2026). **Faction name: the Ancient Eye** (the owner's own name; the doc's "Drowned Choir" is retired and every kind name says "of the Eye"). **Claims: yes** (the act's identity). **Dread: yes**, at the proposed strength (up to +20% damage taken, visual only beyond that). **Unlock gate: Seraphine defeated.** **Finale reward: a 13th summon class, the Spawn of the Deep**, raised from the god; and, the owner added, **Seraphine grants one as well**: the owner asked which class that was, none had been designed, and the recommended **Fallen Seraph** (a hovering corrupted angel in the figure-redesign style that dives on the densest pack and fires fans of black feather blades) was never vetoed, so it is the working choice. **Every female character in the figure-redesign style** ("every female gotta be in that style"), not only the Siren and Merrow's congregation. **Build order: all three stages in parallel after the foundation** (the recommendation was one at a time). **Unlocked Act II spells are offered on every stage** (at the 0.5 card weight); the two counter-passives only in Act II. **A playable character with each new spell** (not a later wave): three more characters, one per abyssal spell, each with one innate and the character unlock rule. Soul Ash ×1.75 / ×2.0 / ×2.25, the stage multipliers and every number stay placeholders for the balance pass. |
| — | Act II foundation (phase 1) | The shared systems every Act II stage builds on, gated by unchanged same-seed summaries on stages 1–3 (checked on 8 configurations: every gameplay line identical; the only differences are informational, the Codex enemy total 23 → 26 and the Sanctum run's "stages unlocked by this run: harbour"). **Stages:** Gullhaven Harbour (`harbour`), the Hollow Archive (`archive`) and Ul-Thalos, the Risen Spire (`spire`) in `scripts/data/stages/`, registered in `StageRegistry` with the new keys `act` (2), `playable` (false: shown as "Coming soon" until built), `dread`, `claims` and `music`; the unlock chain Seraphine → Gullhaven → the Archive → Ul-Thalos; each boss has its own id (`merrow`, `archivist`, `vhalneth`, so a placeholder fight never counts as Seraphine for Hyper or the Codex) and a placeholder `script` until its own `scripts/enemies/eye/boss_<id>.gd` exists (EnemyDirector loads an Eye boss by name). An optional Reckoning `kind` lets a stage send its own Reckoning enemy. The **stage select** shows one act at a time: Act I / Act II tabs (Q / E, LB / RB, click). **The Ancient Eye's kinds:** `EyeEnemyData` (Initiate of the Eye, fodder; Chanter of the Eye, the squad leader that claims; the Drowned Thrall) and one table per stage (`HarbourEnemyData`, `ArchiveEnemyData`, `SpireEnemyData`, empty until phase 2) after the Act I tables in `EnemyDirector.enemy_def()`; Eye kits load by name from `scripts/enemies/eye/<id>_kit.gd`, so the stages add kits without touching the registry. **Claims** (`EyeClaims` as `game.claims`, one path `HordeDirector.claim_fallen()`): a summon dying within a claimer's reach (a `claim_radius`, or a Scribe's mark `Enemy.claimer_until`) rises for 15 s as a hostile Drowned Thrall wearing its sprite tinted drowned teal (HP 0.6× the summon's, speed 0.75×, hit damage from the Thrall row and time-scaled; no XP; at most 8 at once; temporary summons are never claimed); killed, its soul returns as 4 Dread Rally points (`DreadRally.add_points()`); the replenish timers are untouched. **Dread** (`DreadMeter` as `game.dread`, `dread_data.gd`): 0–100 on the necromancer, raised by `add(amount, source)` (the stages' hazards), −1 per kill, +5% damage taken per 25 in `CombatResolver.damage_player()`; Iron Mind's keys `dread_rise_reduction` / `dread_fall_mult` are read when that passive exists; a HUD meter under the HP bar and `DreadVignette` darkening the screen edges (visual only, half as strong and without its pulse under Reduce motion or Effects = Reduced). **Unlocks** (`UnlockData`, the one helper `Unlocks.is_open()` / `offerable()`, Meta `[unlocks]`): an item listed there is offered only once a boss kill or an achievement opened it; a card row's `act` key limits it to one act; the end screen gets a plate per unlocked item; the Codex shows a locked item's condition; the autotest opens items with `--unlocks=all` or by forcing them. **Codex:** an Ancient Eye tab (soldiers, champions, bosses). **Art:** palette ramps `drowned` (darker, bluer and muted against the UI's soul-teal) and `biolume` (magenta-leaning, never the player's violet), the `eye/` sprite folder and ramp set, `EyeGlyphs` fallbacks. **Audio:** "Below the Tide" (`SoundData.TIDE_SCORE` through a score-driven `RunLoopSynth`: A drone, slow glides, a drowned bell every 8 s; the Act I loop is unchanged sample for sample), rendered the first time an Act II run asks for it and never headless. **Autotest:** `--stage=harbour\|archive\|spire`, `--unlocks=all`, and `dread` / `claims` summary lines on Act II stages. |
| — | Fallen Seraph | Owner (Act II proposal answers): "sounds like we need to add one to Seraphine as well"; the recommended **Fallen Seraph** was never vetoed, so it is the working choice. **Unlock:** slaying Grand Inquisitor Seraphine once (any difficulty, any run) unlocks the class `fallen_seraph` and its paired passive `fallen_grace` (two `UnlockData` rows, type class / passive, `boss: "seraphine"`; the end screen shows a plate for each); both are offered on **every stage** (no card-row `act`) at the later-item weight (`shared_card_pool`, 0.5). A fresh save never offers them, so Act I is unchanged. **Retroactive rule (decided here):** a save that slew Seraphine before these rows existed gets them on load: `MetaProgress._load_unlocks()` also opens any boss-unlocked item whose boss's stage record already shows a kill (`_boss_slain_on_record()`, the same rule as Hyper's migration), so it also covers later Act II boss unlocks. **The class** (`UnitData`, behaviour `dive`, `SeraphBehavior`): a hovering corrupted angel in the figure-redesign style, 55 HP, 90 px/s (below Blood Scent's 95: not a fast class), feather blades 12 damage every 1.6 s at 140 px (attack profile `feather`: a bone arrow projectile tagged `fallen_seraph`), 1 → 3 units (Lv 2 dive damage +20%, Lv 4 +15% area, Lv 5 +40% / +20% damage / dive cooldown ÷1.2), replaced every 15 s, 40% of global capacity bonuses, formation `mid` (on the Bone Ring). **Like the Banshee she is not airborne**: drawn 6 px above a shadow, but enemies target and hit her the whole time, the dive included, and the Arena push-out keeps her out of obstacles (`w_threat` 0.7 keeps a little distance between dives). **Dive** every 7 s: the first 10 enemies within 40–220 px of her (spatial-hash order, inside her leash, no structures or Reckoning enemies) are scored by the foes within 40 px of each (an elite / miniboss / the boss counts as a full pack); the best needs 4, else she looks again 0.6 s later. She rises 0.25 s (to 16 px), dives at 380 px/s at that pack-centre enemy (followed while it lives, generation-guarded; at most 0.9 s), strikes every foe within 40 px for 18 (source `seraph_dive`, knockback 110) and lays a **ring of black feathers** (`SeraphFeathers`, pooled, created only on first use) whose front expands from 30% to 85 px in 0.4 s and cuts every foe it passes **once** for 10 (source `seraph_feathers`; a cut list with pooling generations, no instance ids); then she hovers 0.3 s and the shared FSM flies her back to her post. Both sources are minion specials (`MINION_SPECIAL_SOURCES`: crits, Siegecraft, suppression and the chamber penalty apply). No RNG anywhere. Sounds reuse the Gargoyle's takeoff and dive at a higher pitch (no new synth entry, so the startup sound seeds are untouched). **Fallen Grace** (new 5-level passive, army passive): every summon special *_cooldown (Stomp, Dash Strike, Swoop, Deep Freeze, Dirge, Dive) is divided by 1 + 5% → 25%, its own category in `HordeDirector._special_stats()` (exactly ÷1 while not owned). **Seraph of Ruin** (Fallen Seraph Lv 5 + Fallen Grace Lv 5, "transform", chest-delivered after 8:00 like every summon evolution): ×1.3 HP, ×1.2 damage, radius 11; each dive **chains** once to the densest pack within 170 px of the impact (not the one just struck; no rise; `special_time` restarts per dive), and every impact lays **two rings**, the second 0.22 s later and 40% wider. **Volleys:** the class fires fans of 3 / 5 feather blades every 2nd attack (share 0.3, `feather` style, an ash-pale tint since shots are additive); the Seraph of Ruin's showpiece is a 12-feather spiral at every dive impact. No synergy was added. **Art:** `summons/fallen_seraph` (52 px) and `summons/ruin_seraph` (60 px), each with a glide strip and a 24-frame idle strip (robe rig, 2 px bust bounce; the hanging wings rigid), icons `fallen_seraph`, `ruin_seraph` (gold frame) and `fallen_grace`, the dive strip `effects/seraph_dive` (8 frames, `EffectManager.burst()`, art only) and a hand-placed 14 px `projectiles/seraph_feather` for the ring; desaturated ivory, black and ash with a teal glint, never Covenant gold or the player's violet; fallbacks in `SeraphGlyphs`. 7 images, about $0.19. **Autotest:** `--classes=fallen_seraph` (forced = unlocked) and `--passives=fallen_grace`; a `fallen seraph` line in runs that had her (dives, chained dives, searches without a pack, dive hits, rings, feather cuts, damage split blades / dive / feathers, Fallen Grace level); an unlockable class never raised is left out of the `horde:` and `[metric]` class lists, so other runs print as before. **Checks:** default Graveyard, `--policy=army` Graveyard and `--policy=army --stage=sanctum`, and `--mortal` Graveyard seed 4, against the base commit: every gameplay line identical (only the content totals differ: Codex summons 12 → 13, evolutions and recipe rows 23 → 24). `--mortal --policy=summon --classes=fallen_seraph --passives=fallen_grace`: Graveyard seeds 1–3 all victories, min HP 100–111, Alaric killed 61–85 s after arrival, the Seraphs 22% / 25% / 33% of the army's damage (Seraph of Ruin at 8:00–8:41; about half of it the blades and their volleys, a third the rings); Sanctum seeds 1–2 victories, min HP 103–108, Seraphine killed in 47–61 s, the Seraphs 34–36%. The comparable Banshee runs (`--classes=banshee --passives=mournsong`, same seeds): Graveyard 23% / 44%, Sanctum seed 1 a defeat at 18:28. Naive bot with `--unlocks=all`: Graveyard seeds 1–3 never drafted her; Sanctum seed 1 drafted her (unevolved, 22% of the damage, 34 deaths: she dives into the crowd and can be hit). No script errors, `stuck_units=0` and no special ending inside an obstacle in every run. Every number `TUNING`; left for the final balance pass. |
| — | Gullhaven Harbour (Act II phase 2) | Stage 4 built on the foundation's stub (`harbour_stage.gd`, still `"playable": false` until the owner's playtest). **Run:** four clock zones through `MarchDirector` (the Mudflats 20:00, the Fish Market 15:00, the Chapel of the Deep 10:00, the Seawall 5:00; floors `HarbourGround`, twelve circle obstacle kinds), a timeline a step above the Sanctum (enemy HP ×1.15 and damage ×1.22 from 5:00 elapsed, Soul Ash ×1.75; damage ×1.15 left the naive bot no worse off than on the Sanctum), Initiate / Chanter squads for the Claims, Heralds of the Deep (`deep_herald`) as the Reckoning kind with Deep Kin companions, Abbot Merrow at 17:00. **The tide** (`TideDirector`, only when a stage has a `tide` dict): a fixed height field (four sine waves) under a keyframed water level (about 4% of the ground flooded at the start, 40% in the Chapel); in the Seawall only a 230 px half-width strip stays dry; High Water and the Reckoning narrow it further. **Water slows everyone in it by 25%** (the owner's rule) except the swimmers (Deep Kin, the Harbourmaster, the Heralds, Merrow): the necromancer and the summons through one new ground-slow path (`TerrainSlow`: `Necromancer.terrain_slow`, summons through the existing `holy_slow`), enemies through `Enemy.apply_slow()`; ticks every 0.2 s, deterministic. Dry **piers** out to the caches are cut out of the water. **Tide surges** every 4 min from 14:30: a 2.5 s telegraphed band, a wave sweeping it at 650 px/s that shoves summons 60 px, enemies 60 px and the necromancer 40 px (never during Grave Step), then 10 s of flooding. The same water test runs in `tide_water.gdshader` (drawing only). **Bell buoys** (17:30, three, optional; toll Initiate squads every 22 s within 760 px): each one sunk cuts every later scripted wave 12% and the trickle 5% (`HarbourDirector.wave_mult()` / `trickle_mult()`), +8 Soul Ash. **Smugglers' caches** (14:00 ×2, 8:20 ×1) at the end of dry piers drop a treasure chest (placed even at the live-chest cap; +6 Soul Ash). **Kinds** (`HarbourEnemyData`, kits in `scripts/enemies/eye/`): **Deep Kin** (bruiser; a telegraphed 0.85 s leap onto the front rank, 70–230 px, 44 px landing), **Tide Caller** (figure-redesign fishwife; a 150 × 46 px line of water rising 36 px in front of her that throws summons back 80 px), **Netcaster** (a 3 s net that pins one summon through the new generic `SummonedUnit.held_until`; a netted summon is immune for 4 s after; killing the caster cuts it), **the Harbourmaster** miniboss at 9:00 left (1700 HP, always elite; an anchor sweep that drags what it hits toward him and an anchor hurl that reels in the first body it strikes; registered through `set_miniboss()`, the miniboss chest and an XP burst). **Abbot Silas Merrow, Voice of the Deep** (`boss_merrow.gd`, `merrow_data.gd`, 26,000 HP × 1.15): robed, **Sermon** (preaches 4 s; every cultist within 650 px hits ×1.25, walks ×1.2 and takes ×0.85 through the existing `Enemy.buff_*` aura path; losing 4% of his HP breaks it) and **Brine Wave** (a 230 px cone that throws the army back 70 px); below 60% **The Change** (robes shed, ×1.5 speed, faster blows; **Limb Lash**, three lanes, and **Whirl**, a 120 px circle); below 30% **High Water** (the dry strip shrinks to 64 px half-width over 3 s, shorter gaps). Attack poses through `BossPoseData`. **Undertow** (`undertow.gd`, `undertow_data.gd`; unlocked by slaying Merrow, offered on every stage, card weight 0.5 like the later items): a 120° wave toward the aim that hurts and shoves; every third wave leaves 3 s of slowing shallows. **Maelstrom** (+ Salt of the Deep Lv 5, chest at 8:00+): every wave ends in a whirlpool that drags the cone into one knot and grinds it (bosses / minibosses slowed, never dragged). **Salt of the Deep** (new passive, unlocked with Undertow): slowed, chilled or frozen foes take +6% damage a level (+30% at Lv 5), one multiplier in `CombatResolver.damage_enemy()` after Grim Ledger. **Brinewalker** (Act II counter-passive, `"act": 2`, unlocked by the achievement **Low Tide** = a victory on Gullhaven): water and ink slow the necromancer 20% less a level (none at Lv 5), and the summons too from Lv 3, applied once in `TerrainSlow`. **Character:** the **Brine Widow** (figure-redesign style; innate **Riptide**: every second wave leaves shallows; +6% spell area, −5 max HP), unlocked by Undertow Lv 5. **Codex:** the four kinds and the Herald (soldiers / champion), Merrow (bosses), Low Tide; structures are not listed. **Art:** 31 generated images ($0.79 in the ledger): five Eye kinds, Merrow's two forms and two poses, the Brine Widow, bell buoy, cache, 12 obstacle kinds (25 sizes), the mudflats tile (three more floors from existing sources through `make_tile.py`), five badges, the `tide_splash` strip; walk / glide / idle strips by `walk_cycle.py`; stage-select vignette and a sea mood for the menu diorama. **Autotest:** `tide`, `harbour`, `harbour events`, `harbour kinds`, `merrow`, `act II spell` and `act II passives` lines (`HarbourReport`, only when relevant); the bot steps out of the surge band and seeks the buoys / caches. **Tests** (`--mortal --meta=none`, final numbers): army bot 4 of 4 reach 0:00 (seeds 4, 8, 1 and 9 with `--unlocks=all`: min HP 13 / 79 / 105 / 107; Merrow slain in 68–87 s in 3; seed 4's weak picks leave him alive), at damage ×1.15 seeds 0, 5, 6, 7 had min HP 110–116; naive bot 4 of 5 die (12:01, 13:07, 18:16, 18:49), against 2 of 4 on the Sanctum (seeds 1, 2, 3, 5); zero script errors, `stuck_units=0` throughout; the Graveyard and Sanctum same-seed summaries are unchanged in every gameplay line. |
| — | Act II: the Hollow Archive (Stage 5) | Built on the foundation, still `"playable": false` until the lead's integration and the owner's playtest. **Zones** (MarchDirector, `ArchiveStage.ZONES`, own floors `ArchiveGround` and obstacle kinds): the Reading Halls (aligned rows of horizontal shelves: one obstacle per 256 px cell, so the "aisles" are shelf rows with gaps; new optional layout key `orient` in `Arena._generate()`, which keeps each cell's RNG stream the same shape), the Ink Wells (hashed ink wells in the floor, filling and draining at the zone changes), the Orrery (a zone may ask its own clear circle at the change, MarchDirector `clear`; the orrery rises there, rests 6 s, telegraphs its sweep 2 s, turns 45° in 2.5 s, direction alternating; bodies are pushed out of the hub and arms, swept by a turning arm, and slide toward an arm's tip when held against it; a push that would end in an obstacle is skipped, so nobody is trapped; it re-assembles around a necromancer more than 720 px away), the Unwritten Stacks (a rift every 45 s). **Rifts** (`RiftDirector`): 3 s telegraph behind the army (away from the horde's threat direction, else from where he walks), then a breakable rift structure spits the event's squad and two Initiates every 2.5 s; it closes after 10 s, or early when broken (2 Soul Ash). **Forbidden tomes**: five lecterns (2:30, 7:00 ×2, 12:00 ×2), each with a Scribe and three Initiates; standing on one 1.5 s burns it: a free level-up card (`Game.pending_level_ups`) and +20 Dread ("tome"); resealed after 90 s; burning all five sets `archive_all_tomes` and earns **Nothing Unwritten** (MetaData, lifetime `archive_all_tomes_runs`; it unlocks Iron Mind). **Kinds** (`ArchiveEnemyData`, kits by name in `scripts/enemies/eye/`, hazards in `ArchiveHazards`, created on first use on any stage): Scribe of the Unwritten (curses up to 3 uncursed cultists in 130 px for 10 s through `Enemy.claimer_until`), Ink Wraith (`phases`: Arena skips it; rises out of the ink nearest the necromancer at spawn, dives between inks), Pilgrim of the Spore (spore cloud every 4.5 s, a bigger one on death; Dread +10/s inside, summons choke once a tick however many clouds overlap; the first pass, 1.6 dps per overlapping cloud, was half of all summon damage), Gibbering Mass (splits into two Lesser Masses, each into two Least: queued, released next step, fixed offsets), the Librarian (9:00 countdown, 11:00 elapsed: the Index beam, a 1.6 s lane telegraph, turns summons around for 2 s through the new one path `SummonedUnit.misdirect()`, and a tendril lash; `set_miniboss`, miniboss chest), the Unwritten (the Reckoning's `kind`, phasing). Ink: 30% slow through the new one path `Necromancer.apply_ground_slow()` (the place a later Brinewalker reduces), Dread +14/s ("ink"). **The Archivist** (`boss_archivist.gd`, `ArchivistData`, `StageRegistry.BOSS_ARRIVALS["archivist"]`): 20,000 HP × 1.25; Recitation (8/10/13 pages per phase, homing 150 px/s, a hit leaves a 4 s ink pool), Footnotes from 60% (two `ArchivistCopy` enemies the boss owns: targetable, never damaged, never pooled, drawn without a shadow), Erasure from 6 s after arrival (620 px circle, −60 px every 20 s with a 3 s glow, minimum 260; the necromancer and summons are pushed back inside, enemies walk in freely), the Last Page at 25%; attack poses `_cast`, `_split`, `_strike`. **Forbidden Verse** (`ForbiddenVerse`, `VerseData`, unlocked by the Archivist with Marginalia): 3 pages orbit (64-80 px), a sweep every 5-4 s, cuts inscribe (`Enemy.inscribed_until`, written only by the spell), an inscribed death bursts (burst × damage in 40-50 px, source `ink_burst`); first pass (burst 1.6-2.0, radius 46-60) was ~80% of the spell's 260k damage, cut. **The Unwritten Word** (Marginalia Lv 5): 6 pages, each burst may set off one more (chain ≤ 6 links). **Marginalia**: +8%/level on `VerseData.MARGINALIA_SOURCES` in `CombatResolver.damage_enemy()`; it is locked with the spell so Act I card pools stay unchanged. **Iron Mind**: `"act": 2`, the foundation's keys (`dread_rise_reduction` 0.1-0.5, `dread_fall_mult` 1 then 2 at Lv 5). **The Heretic Scribe** (Forbidden Verse; innate Illuminated Margins `verse_burst_radius` +30%; −10 HP, +5% speed; male, so no figure-redesign rig). Autotest: an `--spells=` spell's paired passive counts as forced for the unlock check (`GameState.autotest_forces()`). Balance: a step harder than Gullhaven (enemy HP ×1.25 and damage ×1.2 from 5:00, rates 1.6→5.8/s, caps 34→164, ×2.0 Soul Ash). `--policy=army --mortal --meta=none`, seeds 1–6 (final data): 6/6 reach 0:00, min HP 17–110, the Archivist slain in 5 of 6 (+46 to +94 s), the Librarian slain in all (11:32–11:51), build complete 12:52–14:55, 5–6 chests, tomes 2–5 of 5; seed 7 is a weak-hand spiral (passives picked over classes, the army stays at 4, Claims raise 400+ thralls) and dies at 17:28. Naive (`--mortal --meta=none`, seeds 11–16): 1 of 6 reaches 0:00 (deaths 10:49–18:10, most after the Archivist arrives with incomplete builds), above the Act I naive target, left for the lead's 16-seed check. Tuning from the first pass: XP of the new kinds cut by 1 each (builds were complete by ~12:45), one elite in three event groups (4–5 chests to 5–6), Ink Wraith claws 5→4 and army target priority 0.5→0.7, page damage to the necromancer 8→6, spore clouds once per summon a tick at 0.8 dps. Stages 1–3: same-seed summaries unchanged (only the catalog totals grow) |
| — | Ul-Thalos, the Risen Spire | Stage 6, the finale of Act II (owner-approved proposal, built in phase 2; `spire_stage.gd`, still `"playable": false` until the lead's Act II gate). **Zones on the clock** through `MarchDirector`: the Breakwater (20:00), the Sunken Steps (15:00), the Dream Gate (10:00), the Spire (5:00), each with its floor style (`SpireGround`: tiles `tiles/spire_<style>`, code decals) and cyclopean obstacle kinds (`SpireTerrainGlyphs` fallbacks; ids `cyclopean_block`, `barnacle_pillar`, `fallen_idol`, `step_ruin`, `sunken_idol` (not `drowned_idol`, which Gullhaven uses), `gate_pylon`, `toppled_arch`, `spire_shard`). **The sinking** (`SinkingDirector`, `SINKING`; one path): the island is a circle around the spire (the world origin) of each zone's `shore` radius (1650 / 1250 / 880 / 520 px); the new shoreline is telegraphed 8 s before the zone change (a biolume ring, never gold, never the player's violet), then the sea rises over 20 s; at 0:00 the Waking floods it to 320 px over 45 s. Deep water works like the edge of the world: `Arena.resolve_all()` ends with `SinkingDirector.contain_all()` (the necromancer, every non-airborne summon, every enemy except Vhal'Neth) and the drops (crystals, power-ups, chests) are moved onto the stone every 6 ticks, so nothing is ever left in the sea and the autotest's stuck check stays 0; `Arena.free_point()` clamps through the shore, the obstacle generator rejects a centre within 70 px of the shoreline being flooded to, and every Eye spawn climbs out of the sea at the shore (`spawn_point()`: 26 px inside, at least 300 px from the necromancer, walking around the coast). **Dream pillars** (`SpireDirector`, the "pillars" event at 10:25): three optional `dream_pillar` structures 240–330 px from the centre (inside the last shoreline), each guarded by a Hierophant; standing within 220 px of one adds 2.5 Dread / s (source "pillar"); each one toppled pays 30 XP in 6 crystals and 8 Soul Ash (`objective_ash`); each one still standing when she arrives gives Vhal'Neth's Dreaming phase one more arm; no miniboss. **Guests:** the kinds of Gullhaven and the Archive join as guest squads (every 80 s from 2:30 to 16:40) when `EnemyDirector.is_known_kind()` finds them (candidate ids per kind), so the stage needs no edit when those tables merge. **Kinds** (`spire_enemy_data.gd`, one kit each in `scripts/enemies/eye/`, Codex soldier entries): the **Siren of the Eye** (elite, female, figure-redesign style; her telegraphed 1.2 s song (every 15 s, only with 3+ undead near) lures up to 6 undead within 190 px out of the Bone Ring toward her for 2 s through the one `SummonedUnit.compel()` path), the **Spawn of the Deep** (a heavy: a 0.6 s tentacle reach, then it holds one summon for 3 s through `compel()`, drags it in and squeezes it; its death frees the summon), the **Hierophant of the Eye** (elite leader that claims; **Ascension**: a 12 s channel in a biolume pillar, broken by 25% of its HP lost during it, after which it walks and retries; finished, it becomes an elite Spawn of the Deep) and the **Dreamspawn of the Waking** (the Reckoning kind). Every number is TUNING (not in GDD). |
| — | Vhal'Neth, the Dreaming Mother and the ending | The Act II final boss (`boss_vhalneth.gd`, hazards in `vhalneth_hazards.gd`, arms in `arm_kit.gd`, data in `vhalneth_data.gd`, `StageRegistry.BOSS_ARRIVALS` "vhalneth" at 17:00; Codex boss entry). 20,000 HP × the stage's 1.25. **Dreaming:** eyes shut, submerged 170 px beyond the shore and `untargetable` (left out of the target grids); 2 arms + 1 per standing pillar rise at the shore (each 8% of her HP) and slam telegraphed lanes at the necromancer or the army's centre; severing them deals 30% of her HP in wounds, the last one ends the phase. **Stirring:** her face rises just past the shore (in melee reach) and glides along the coast after the necromancer; her **gaze** (every 10 s) opens a 120 px eye over his path for 1.4 s, then rests 5 s and drifts: inside, Dread +14 / s (`game.dread.add(..., "gaze")`; on a stage without Dread a small damage tick instead) and the undead inside wither; 2 arms keep slamming and regrow 16 s after being severed (each a small wound). **Waking (35%):** she rises onto the island (the face is the weak point, damage taken × 1.4), ringed by 4 arms, slams faster, gazes every 7 s and **roars** (every 11 s: a 1.8 s telegraphed 165 px nova that throws the army back). **The ending:** on her death the finale plays (the binding rings, the arms sink) and ENDING_ANNOUNCE "THE DEAD GOD SERVES / The necromancer raises Vhal'Neth" is the defeat banner; the Reckoning becomes **the Waking** (its own kind and a last flood). The achievement **The Dead God Serves** (boss kill on Ul-Thalos; bounty 300) opens the **campaign epilogue** covering both acts (`epilogue_data.gd`, `EpilogueScreen`, main menu item "Epilogue" above Credits once any part is open; its Act I part opens with *Dawn Extinguished*, answering the open "campaign epilogue that Dawn Extinguished should unlock"). |
| — | Call of the Drowned, Abyssal Pact and the Tide Widow | The Act II spell unlocked by slaying Vhal'Neth (`UnlockData`; a later item, offered on every stage once unlocked; one `NecroSpellBook` dispatch, `call_of_the_drowned.gd`, fixed rules in `call_drowned_data.gd`). Every 6 s (5 s at Lv 5) 1–3 tendrils mark the strongest foes within 280–320 px, erupt 0.45 s later (26–58 damage in a 34–44 px circle, slow 50% for 1.5 s) and a foe they kill rises as a **Drowned Thrall** ally (a Warrior temporary through the one `HordeDirector.spawn_temporary()` path: 12 s, HP 0.5–0.7×, damage 0.6–1.0×, at most 6; Restless Dead and Restless multiply as on every temporary). **Tidecaller's Host** (Lv 5 + Abyssal Pact Lv 5, chest-delivered like every evolution): 3 tendrils every 4.5 s, 80 damage, and the drowned rise as Spawns of the Deep (14 s) that seize foes. **Abyssal Pact** (new passive, unlocked by Vhal'Neth): every temporary summon releases a soul burst when it crumbles, healing the undead within 70–110 px by 6–20 (`AbyssalPact.on_temporary_gone()` from `HordeDirector.on_unit_died()`). **The Tide Widow** (its playable character, figure-redesign style, unlocked the usual way): innate *Drowned Chorus* (Thralls +40% HP, +2 cap, read inside the spell through `game.character.innate_value()`), +10 max HP, −5% move speed; Hyper skin the Gilded Tide Widow. |
| — | Spawn of the Deep class and the Leviathan | The 13th offerable summon class (owner-approved proposal decision 5), unlocked by slaying Vhal'Neth, a later item (card weight 0.5), offered on every stage once unlocked. A slow front-line heavy (210 HP, 14 damage, blocks like a wide warrior, Grave Dominion V gives it +2 only) whose `SummonBehavior` "grasp" (`deep_spawn_behavior.gd`) reaches 90 px with a tentacle, holds one foe for 2.4 s (through `CombatResolver.stun_enemy()`, so bosses and structures obey the usual stun rules), drags it in and squeezes it (source `deep_grasp`, a minion special for every summon passive); its own volley (`VolleyData`). **Leviathan** (Spawn Lv 5 + new passive **Crushing Depths** Lv 5, "transform"): HP × 1.4, damage × 1.25, seizes two foes at once and sweeps 80 px around it with every grasp (source `leviathan_sweep`, knockback); its volley is the showpiece. **Crushing Depths** (unlocked by Vhal'Neth, an army passive): summons deal +8–40% damage to foes that are held, stunned or frozen (one check in `CombatResolver.damage_enemy()` after Siegecraft, stat `crushing_depths_bonus_dmg`). Codex summon entry; card and autotest text in `summon_behaviors.gd`. |
| — | Ul-Thalos balance and art (Act II phase 2) | Measured with `--mortal --meta=none` on the final data, eight army seeds (1337, 2–8) and four naive seeds (1337, 2–4). **Army:** 7 of 8 reach 0:00 and slay Vhal'Neth (min HP before 0:00 56 / 97 / 109 / 107 / 74 / 111 / 70 in the wins; she falls 51–153 s after arriving, five of seven within 1:45); seed 7 dies at 17:36: its bot opens with priests and passives, its army is 3 of 6 at 2:00 and never recovers. **Naive:** 4 of 4 die (11:27–17:36; an earlier pass: 3 of 4). The stage stays a step above the Sanctum: enemy HP and damage × 1.25 from 5:00 (Sanctum × 1.1), Soul Ash × 2.25. Nine balance passes from the first run (the army wiped in the boss fight, 2 / 45 alive, Vhal'Neth at 15k HP): the Spawns of the Deep's trickle −40% and their squeeze 4 → 2.5 (cooldown 7 → 9 s), contact 10 → 7; the gaze 3 → 1 per summon tick (it rests on the Bone Ring), slams on summons 14 → 9 and on the necromancer 18 → 12, the roar 20 → 10 with a 1.8 s telegraph and 165 px; Vhal'Neth 34,000 → 20,000 HP; the Hierophant channels 10 s after arriving (not 4: it climbs out at the shore) and breaks at 25% HP lost; the Siren 150 → 120 HP, songs every 15 s, only with 3+ undead near, at most 6 lured (a weak army could not kill the Sirens and they sang 300 times); a gentler opening (no Chanter in the first minute, the first Chanter squad at 1:15, the Breakwater's density near the Sanctum's) and a softer step into the Sunken Steps (2 Spawns, a 16 + 1 ring). **Art:** 44 API images (about $0.76 at the tool's estimate, logged in the main checkout's ledger): every new unit (the Siren and the Tide Widow in the figure-redesign style with bust glide / walk and idle breathing strips), Vhal'Neth's three faces, the four floors and the sea (`make_tile.py` recipes), 8 obstacle kinds in all their sizes (the vertical toppled arch keeps its code glyph), 7 badges, 3 effect strips (`AnimData`), the Gilded Tide Widow; the decals and the sinking's water band stay code-drawn; the stage-select vignette is composed from this art. |

| — | Act II integration (phase 2) | The four parallel workstreams (Gullhaven Harbour, the Hollow Archive, Ul-Thalos with the Spawn of the Deep, the Fallen Seraph) merged on `integration`; their own rows above hold each design. Merge fixes: the Archive's ink slow goes through `TerrainSlow` (the one ground-slow path) so Brinewalker cuts it as designed; Call of the Drowned sits in `NecroSpellBook.ACT2_IDS` (its own report line) instead of the Act I "later spells" line, whose text stays unchanged; the Ancient Eye's Codex lists hold all three stages. Gate: same-seed summaries on stages 1–3 identical on every gameplay line (8 configurations; only catalog totals grew: recipes 28, Codex 15 / 14 / 41 / 6, skins 34). **Balance** (fresh save, `--mortal`, 8 seeds per stage and bot): the first batch had the army bot reach 0:00 in 23 of 24 runs but the naive bot die in 6/8 (Gullhaven), 8/8 (the Archive) and 7/8 (Ul-Thalos), its top killer everywhere the Initiate swarm and a losing army feeding Claims. Changes: Initiate damage 3.5 → 3.0 (the Recruit's), Claims at most 5 Thralls (8) within a Chanter's 90 px (120); the Archive's and Ul-Thalos's stage multipliers ×1.25 → ×1.2 from 7:00 (5:00); the Archivist's pages 6 → 4.5 to the necromancer and the Gibbering Mass's crush 10 → 8 (the two top killers of the builds that died to her). Final: army bot 8/8 / 8/8 / 8/8 reach 0:00 (min HP 100 / 68 / 106; bosses killed 8/8 / 8/8 / 8/8, 73–93 s median); naive bot, fresh save, dies 3/8 / 7/8 / 8/8, which is the wrong yardstick for an act opened only by slaying Seraphine; **naive bot with mid Ossuary upgrades** (`--meta=mid`, 6 seeds): Sanctum 0/6 deaths (min HP 87), Gullhaven 0/6 (70), the Archive 2/6, both after 19:20 (lowest 0), Ul-Thalos 0/6 (21; Vhal'Neth killed in 1/6): a climb, with the Archive's boss fight still the sharpest spike. The three stages are now **playable**; the owner's playtest decides the rest. |
| — | Act II art review (owner, Oct 2026) | The owner reviewed all 207 new Act II and volley images on a review page (OK / Redo with notes): 190 OK, 13 Redo, one note. Redone: **Abbot Merrow** (still and sermon pose) is now an ordinary elderly human priest before The Change ("more human, so the change is horrific"), his changed form untouched; the **Herald of the Deep** keeps its design with fewer, bigger details ("our enemies are so small: make the details larger"); the **Harbourmaster** keeps everything but gets a Deep Kin fish face; the **bell buoy**'s bell hangs free in an open frame (room to swing); the **chapel** floor is hardwood planks (a village chapel's floor), not flagstones; the **lobster pots and net piles** are drawn pixel by pixel by `tools/mesh_props.py` (an AI mesh cannot survive the reduction to 20-29 px and read as chests / sand); the **bust bounce** of the Siren of the Eye and the Gilded Tide Widow sat on the waist and now sits on the chest (Siren rig box raised, `walk_cycle.py SKIN_FIX` for the Hyper skin, whose crown had pushed the derived box 2 rows down). **Round 2** (the 14 redone images and 3 unmarked ones): 14 OK; the **Scribe** now has one huge eye for a head inside his hood ("his face looks messed up"); the Gilded Tide Widow's bounce box is 2 px wider and uses the new optional **two-lobe** bounce (`bust` field 6 = 2: one bell per side instead of one across the box, whose outer side barely moved: "just one is bouncing"). Visual only; no gameplay change. |
| — | GitHub Pages is the web home (owner, Oct 2026) | The owner dropped itch.io ("with all the issues with itch, we should give up on publishing there"): the `publish-itch` CI job is gone, and the Web build lives at <https://smoketsu.github.io/NecropathyPlay/> (`publish-pages-repo`: a public build-only repository, `PAGES_REPO` + `PAGES_TOKEN`; from `main` pushes, `v*` tags and manual runs of `main`), the build iPhone Safari keeps saves for. **Play button** (owner report: no sound at all on the Pages build in Microsoft Edge): the page (`web/mobile_shell.html`) downloads the engine and the pack at once but starts the engine only on a click / tap / key on a **Play** button under the NECROPATHY logo. Godot creates its AudioContext while it starts, so the context is born inside the gesture and runs at once in every browser (on itch.io the "Run game" click had always come first). The silence itself turned out to be the bus bug in the next row, not Edge. The project's boot splash is now the NECROPATHY logo on the game ground (desktop builds show it too). CI artifacts are kept 7 days (test logs 3) after a full artifact store blocked publishing. Earlier rows that name itch.io describe the history. |
| — | Web sound fix (owner report, Oct 2026) | The owner still heard nothing in Edge after the Play button. Measuring the page's real output in Chromium (a tap on everything sent to the speakers, checked with a test tone) showed the Web build was **silent in every browser**: Godot 4.3's Web player mirrors `AudioServer.add_bus()` (sent as "at -1" even with an explicit end position) through `GodotAudio.Bus.addAt` / `move`, whose `splice(-2)` puts the new bus BEFORE Master. Its bus order then differed from the engine's, and `set_bus_send(Music / SFX, "Master")` wired Master into Music and SFX: a loop without a delay, which Web Audio mutes, and nothing connected to the speakers. Desktop builds never use that code. Fix: `Sfx._setup_buses()` appends the buses with `AudioServer.set_bus_count()` (mirrored by `Bus.setCount`, which appends in order); same buses on desktop, same-seed autotest summaries unchanged. Rule: never call `add_bus()` in this project. After the fix the UI clicks and the menu theme reach the output. |
| — | Volleys kept (owner, Oct 2026) | After playing it the owner keeps the bullet heaven pass ("volleys are great, makes the screen much more visually interesting"), which answers the "kept or dropped" question of the "Bullet heaven pass" row. It stays on by default; the Settings → Video → Volleys toggle and `--volleys=off` stay for players and A/B tests. |
| — | Content wave 2 (owner decisions, Oct 2026) | The owner approved the five proposals of the Claude Docs proposal "Necropathy: Content Proposals (Oct 2026)" with every recommendation but one: (1) a **victory screen**: a ~3 s last stand after the Reckoning (instant with Reduce motion / the autotest), a title and a boss line per stage, a victory results card with stage seal, best time past 0:00, highlights and Next / Again / Menu; (2) **achievement rewards**: options and cosmetics only, never raw power (the Ossuary stays the only strength), each keeps its Soul Ash bounty; Bone Legion → the Bone Golem, Heretic's Bane → title + portrait frame, Dread Sovereign → ultimate Sovereign's Decree, Dawn Extinguished → ultimate Eclipse, The Dead God Serves → title; five new achievements (Mass Grave → ultimate Grave Tide, Untouchable → ghost skin, Full Ossuary → title, Deathlord's Due → crowned skin, Swift Judgment → title), all through `UnlockData` / `Unlocks`; titles shown on the pre-run screen and the victory card; (3) the **illustrated epilogue**: 7 pixel-art panels for the existing two-part epilogue, played once after the first clear (skippable, replayable), a slow "Grave Lullaby" cue, a sequel-hint card; (4) **more ultimates**: Sovereign's Decree, Eclipse and Grave Tide beside Dread Rally (the default), one shared 240-point meter, picked on the pre-run screen for any character, never stronger than Dread Rally (A/B through `--ultimate=`), no freeze or stun on bosses; (5) the **Bone Golem**: a 15th summon class (locked until Bone Legion): a slow anchor (1, 2 at Lv 5), Ossuary Hunger (absorbs fallen summons' bones within 140 px: +4% max HP each, up to +60%), Bulwark (plants 3 s every 12 s: solid, draws enemies within 120 px), a slam shard ring volley; evolution **Ossuary Titan** with the new passive **Grave Masonry**; synergy pair **Charnel House** (Golem + Abomination) and the Menagerie. **The one change:** no Act II Claims denial for the Golem (owner: "this makes bone golem only good in act 2 ... we want our summons to remain useful"); a rule for every summon from here on: no act-specific summon mechanics. All new art is reviewed by the owner at the end on a review page, as for Act II. |
| — | Achievement rewards (content wave 2) | Built from Proposal 2 (workstream B). **One reward path:** `UnlockData` gains three reward types (`REWARD_TYPES`): `ultimate` (rows `sovereigns_decree` ← Dread Sovereign, `eclipse` ← Dawn Extinguished, `grave_tide` ← Mass Grave; the ultimates themselves are workstream C's, its picker asks `Unlocks.is_open(id)`), `title` (`heretics_bane`, `master_of_the_drowned` ← The Dead God Serves, `archnecromancer` ← Full Ossuary, `executioner` ← Swift Judgment) and `skin` (the universal families `untouchable`, `deathlord`); `Unlocks.is_open()` stays THE check (an id absent from `UnlockData` is open), `Unlocks.item_name()` names any item. The autotest's `--unlocks=all` opens only the card pool (spells, passives, classes), not rewards. A save that earned an achievement before its reward row existed has the reward at load (`MetaProgress._load_unlocks()`, like the old-boss rule). **Titles** (`TitleData`): purely cosmetic, worn under the character's name on the pre-run hero and (at integration) on the victory card through `TitleData.display_text(Meta.selected_title())`; chosen on the pre-run screen with a small cycle row (`PreRunTitleRow`: None, then each earned title; a free choice saved at once as Meta `[prerun] title`; its detail line shows "n/4 · next: <title>", the tooltip and the Codex say how to earn each). Heretic's Bane brings the **gold Chaplain-skull frame** (`TitleFrame`, drawn in code on the pixel grid: a chamfered gold band, a skull on each corner, a Chaplain's skull under a holy cross at the top) around the hero while worn. **Skins for every character** (`SkinData.UNIVERSAL_SKINS`): "Untouchable" (over-bright cold-white multiply, see-through) and "Deathlord" (black iron, the crimson-jewelled gold crown emblem), generated as `<character>_untouchable` / `_deathlord`, palette variants through `SkinGlyphs` / `SpriteBank.draw()`'s tint, never a stat; the skin row uses compact tiles when a character has more than 3 skins (`FrontDoorData.SKIN_TILES_FULL`). **Five new achievements** (`MetaData`, bounties 150-250 in line with the others): *Mass Grave* (500 temporary undead in one run: `HordeDirector.temp_spawned`, the one `spawn_temporary()` counter; lifetime best `best_temp_raised`), *Untouchable* (reach 0:00 never left below 50% max HP by a hit before 0:00, a killing hit survived by Undying Will counts as 0%: `CombatResolver.damage_player()` → `RunRewards.note_player_hit()`), *Full Ossuary* (4 evolutions, spell or summon, in one run: the `evolutions` stat, never lost within a run), *Deathlord's Due* (a victory on Deathlord), *Swift Judgment* (the stage boss slain within 45 s of `EnemyDirector.boss_spawned`). `RunRewards` (`game.rewards`) only reads the run and hands its counters to the summary as `run_rewards` (not the stats, so the stats line is unchanged); `MetaProgress.run_counters()` turns them into five lifetime counters checked in `award_run()`. **Under the autotest** the five are checked only with `--meta-save` or the new `--rewards=on` (in memory), so default summaries (bounty, Soul Ash) are unchanged; the new last line `rewards:` lists the achievements earned, titles open, reward items unlocked, the run counters and which one-run achievements the run met. **Shown:** end-screen plates "Ultimate unlocked" / "Title earned" / "Skin unlocked" (`UnlockData.PLATE_HEADINGS`; a universal skin is one plate, not one per character); the Codex reward column now reads each achievement's `unlock` line (every one filled: Bone Legion → Bone Golem + Ivory Seer, Heretic's Bane → title + frame, Dread Sovereign → Sovereign's Decree, Dawn Extinguished → Eclipse + Act I epilogue, The Dead God Serves → Master of the Drowned + Act II epilogue) plus the bounty, with a skin preview for the skin rewards; new badges use existing icons as fallbacks (no new PNGs). Same-seed gate (seed 7, Graveyard default and Gullhaven Harbour army vs e0a3eb5): every summary line identical except the `skins:` line's two skin counts (34 → 64 skins, 4 → 6 setup row cards: the universal skins) and the new `rewards:` line. |
| — | Victory screen (content wave 2) | Built as approved (Proposal 1; texts, timings and sizes in `victory_data.gd`). **Last stand:** `Game.end_run()` counts the death, builds the summary, pays the Soul Ash and freezes the world exactly as before; then `LastStand` plays ~3 s on real time, drawing only: the frozen screen drains of colour (eased out, so it "slows to a stop"; one full-screen pass, `victory_wash.gdshader`) and the HUD fades, the surviving summons turn outward and kneel (each body layer crouched about its feet and mirrored away from the necromancer, nearest first, rippling out), a gold wash rises, the stage title card fades in above the ring; any key / button / click / tap skips; skipped with Reduce motion and under the autotest; the defeat flow is unchanged. Decided here: the run is never stepped again after the death (no literal slow motion, which would move gameplay), so the "slowing" is the colour freeze. **Card:** the victory path of `end_screen.gd` (`VictoryResults` for the numbers, `VictoryCard` for the widgets): the stage title on the ribbon; a slot for the player's title under it (`EndScreen.player_title`, empty and hidden until the achievement-reward titles fill it); ONE line under it: the stage's boss line when the boss fell, else the act's line ("You outlasted the Covenant / the Eye. The Reckoning claimed you at last."; decided here: one line keeps the card inside 1280 × 600); the stage seal (the stage-select vignette, violet frame; gold frame, gold wash and the Hyper badge on a Hyper clear); the time past 0:00 (HERO), the run time and the stage's **Best** with a "New record" ribbon (a first clear with time past 0:00 is a record); enemies slain and level; highlights: top-damage summon class (its `dmg_<source>` stats mapped to the class), up to 3 evolutions with their game times (+N more), chests opened, the largest army (new `Game.peak_army`), the boss kill time (new `Game.boss_kill_time`); the shared unlock plates and Soul Ash column; buttons **Next** (N / pad Y: the next `StageRegistry.ORDER` stage with the same `RunConfig`, shown only when it is unlocked, after this run's award, and playable), **Again** (R / pad X), **Menu** (Esc / pad B). The new counters ride in the summary (`boss_kill_time`, `peak_army`, `evolution_log`), never in `stats`, so every existing autotest line is unchanged. **Record:** Meta's `[stages]` record gains `best_reckoning` (the most seconds past 0:00 in a victory there; additive key, 0 when absent; a save from before `[stages]` gets the lifetime best on the Graveyard), written only by `award_run()`'s usual save, never under the autotest. **Debug:** `--victory-preview` (GameState) skips the menu and ends a short run as a scripted victory for screenshots; it never saves (`Meta.can_write()`). Autotest: a `victory screen` line on every victory. |
| — | Bone Golem (content wave 2) | Proposal 5 of the "Content wave 2" row, built (workstream D). **Class `bone_golem`** (the 15th class, locked until **Bone Legion** through `UnlockData` rows `bone_golem` / `grave_masonry`, condition `"achievement": "bone_legion"`; a fresh save offers 12 classes as before, and stages 1–6 keep their same-seed summaries): a slow anchor (1 body, 2 at Lv 5; Grave Dominion gives it a 20% share, +1 at V), 420 HP, 48 px/s, radius 18 (block ring 22, mass 10), formation "front" on the Bone Ring; it lumbers 1.7× faster only to rejoin its post (`rejoin_speed_mult`, a new optional UnitData key on the catch-up and return speeds; the first forced tests at its fighting speed left it 60% of its time without a target). Target profile "fortress" (nearest foes first, not the guard profile's danger filter). Attack: a two-handed **slam** (32 dmg every 2.2 s, 28 px reach, 0.35 s windup) on the shared melee path plus a splash of 70% to every other foe within 40 px (`GolemBehavior.on_melee_hit()`, source `golem_slam`). **Ossuary Hunger**: a summon of yours that dies within 140 px (a real death, or a temporary summon that was killed, not one that crumbled) is absorbed by the nearest Golem (`GolemKit`, `HordeDirector.golems`, created by the first Golem): +4% max HP each, up to +60%, a per-unit multiplier applied in `SummonedUnit.apply_stats()` through the new generic `SummonBehavior.adjust_stats()` hook (HP % kept, GDD §34), and the body drawn up to ×1.25 (`UnitMotion.set_size()`, visual only). **Bulwark**: every 12 s with 3+ foes within 80 px (or an elite / miniboss / the boss) it plants for 3 s: rooted but still thinking and slamming (`SummonBehavior.rooted()`, not a CASTING special), block mass 400 (the existing blocker push-out on the spatial hash, no physics engine) and a 120 px taunt through ColossusKit's taunt list (`SummonBehavior.taunt_reach()`; bosses, Reckoning foes, structures ignore it; counted as `golem_taunts`); while planted, foes in slam reach score +2 and the others −1. Volley: from Lv 3 each slam throws a ring of 8 (Lv 5: 12) bone shards. **Passive `grave_masonry`**: the blockers (`PassiveData.MASONRY_CLASSES`: Warriors, Abominations, Golems and their evolutions) take 5% → 18% less damage (`CombatResolver.damage_unit()`, its own category after Unholy Resilience) and hold 10% → 30% longer (block mass and the Bulwark time, `compute_stats()` after the evolution overlay). **Evolution `ossuary_titan`** (chest, Golem Lv 5 + Grave Masonry Lv 5, past 8:00): ×1.4 HP, ×1.3 damage, radius 19 / ring 23 / mass 12, slam radius +25%; each absorbed body heals it 8% of its max HP and, at most every 2 s, gives every summon within 110 px an 18 HP barrier (`SummonAbilities.grant_barrier()`, the Warder's rule: the stronger barrier wins); when it falls it collapses into a straight wall of 7 Bone Wall segments for 6 s across the line from the necromancer to it (`BoneWall.raise_wall()`: the spell's segment system and push-out, no segment in an obstacle, bosses / minibosses / Reckoning foes / structures not held; spikes 30% of its damage per tick, source `titan_wall`; the Bone Wall node is created when missing and only ages such walls while the spell is not learned, `NecroSpell.idle_step()`); showpiece volley: a spiral of 16 bone shards per slam. **Synergy `charnel_house`** (Golem + Abomination, Lv 2 each): a slam marks foes *slammed* for 6 s (one stomp cooldown) (`Enemy.slammed_until`, written only while it is active), Abomination / Bone Colossus stomps deal +50% to slammed foes through `SynergyManager.hit_mult()` (one mark bonus per hit, half on the stage boss); the Golem joins `NEWER_CLASSES` (it counts toward the Charnel Menagerie and takes its bonuses); its numbers print on the autotest's `bone golem` line, not the `synergy bonus` line. Open: its lift is small (a forced Titan + Abomination Sanctum run: 264 slammed marks, 27 bonus stomp hits, +358 damage; the same at 4 s and 6 s, as slammed foes die or are stomped at once), a pair screen or an aim hook (stomps preferring slammed foes) is left for the owner's playtest. No act-specific rule (owner decision). Balance screen (4 seeds × Graveyard / Sanctum, `--mortal --policy=army`, `--classes=bone_golem,warrior,archer,mage` vs the same with `abomination`): at first (26 dmg, 130 px reach, 60% splash) the Golem was as safe but dealt 42–57% of the Abomination's damage; tuned to 32 / 160 px / 70%: 8/8 runs reach 0:00 with no death and kill the boss (as the Abomination), min HP before 0:00 97 / 92 (Abomination 100 / 92), class damage 66% / 86% of the Abomination's, the class dies 1.5 times a run (Abomination 4.5): a solid, sturdier, lower-damage frontline option, not a must-pick. Art (owner review pending): Golem 88×80 and Titan 104×104 stills with walk strips, the class, Titan, passive and synergy badges, the `golem_slam` effect strip. |
| — | Illustrated epilogue (content wave 2) | Proposal 3 as approved. **Slides:** `EpilogueScreen` is a cutscene over an opaque black backdrop (the menu's scrim taming does not reach it): one slide per paragraph, the part's heading (HEADER, gold) and act + "n / N" (LABEL) above a framed 944×424 picture window, the paragraph (NAME) fading in under it (0.5 s delay, 1.1 s fade), a 0.9 s picture crossfade; a slide lasts 6 s + 0.045 s per character (about 12–14 s) and moves on by itself, the last one waits; **any key, pad button, click or tap** moves on (0.3 s guard so a tap's touch + emulated click count once), **Esc / pad B or Skip** (a top-right button that never takes focus, for touch) ends it; keys are taken before the GUI so no menu button underneath reacts. Reduce motion: no pan (the pan's middle), instant fades. Fits 1280×600 layout px (~590 px tall, `TouchSupport.fit_to_screen()`). **Panels:** 7 pixel-art panels (Act I: the Sanctum burning with the shattered relic; the dying sun over the pilgrim road and its column of dead; the army descending the cliffs toward the glow under the sea. Act II: Gullhaven with the tide pulled back and the Eye's faithful praying on the pier; the Archive's last page burning before the kneeling Archivist; Ul-Thalos sinking; Vhal'Neth bowing to the necromancer) plus an 8th for the **closing card** "The Beacons Burn" (after Act II only: the other holy orders, under white-sun, blue-star and grey-sword banners and blue cloaks, never Covenant crimson, march out of a fortress while beacons burn; "To be continued."). They are 1024×576 art px drawn 1 art px = 1 layout px through the sharp-pixel shader (the game's grid rule, the menu diorama's scale), each with its own slow pan (smoothstep, the whole slide; `EpilogueData.PARTS` "panels"). A sealed part is one "Sealed" slide with its goal over its first panel as a dark silhouette (×0.08–0.12, the Codex's convention). **Autoplay once:** when the player returns to the main menu **from a run**, every part that is open and not seen yet plays over the menu (`EpilogueScreen.play_unseen()`, from `Main._show_menu()`); a part shown (autoplay or the menu item) is marked in Meta `[epilogue] seen_<act1/act2>` (additive keys, never written under the autotest). Decision: an older save that already earned a part has no seen flag, so it sees the illustrated part once after its next run; Restart never plays it (only the menu). **Music:** a variant of "Grave Lullaby" rendered by the same `MenuThemeSynth` (new optional `variant`: tempo, a shorter loop of the first bars, muted layers; with none it is bit-identical, checked by md5 against the base): section A only at 56.25 BPM (3/4), no timpani, harp or crackle, a 34.1 s loop (0.75 s compute), on its own player on the Music bus (no new bus), `Sfx.MUSIC_EPILOGUE` crossfades with the menu theme / run loops (2.5 s in, 1.5 s out); headless never renders it; on the Web it steps per frame only while the epilogue plays and fades in once ready (the slides never wait). **Debug:** `-- --epilogue` (real state) / `--epilogue=all` (every part as if open, nothing saved) opens it at startup. Art made with `tools/epilogue_panel.py` (16:9 crop, 3×3 mode filter, box downscale ×1/1.5, 128 median-cut / k-means clusters snapped to the locked palette, lone-pixel cleanup; per-panel ramp drops recorded in the tool so `--all` rebuilds exactly); sources and prompts in `art_source/epilogue/`. All numbers `TUNING`. |
| — | Bone Bastion (owner decision, Oct 2026) | It replaces the Golem's Charnel House pair (a slam mark that Abomination stomps cashed in): in merged runs it almost never fired, because Golems and Abominations hold different Bone Ring posts (one forced run: 27 bonus hits; letting a slammed foe trigger a stomp made it worse, 214 → 141 bonus damage). The owner chose a defensive aura instead, **Bone Bastion**: a Bone Golem + **any blocker** (a Warrior or an Abomination, evolved forms included), each Lv 2. Every OTHER summon within 120 px of a live Golem takes 15% less damage, 30% while that Golem is planted (Bulwark); with Grave Masonry a hit is cut by at most 60% (`max_combined`).
